# Battle scenario reports (mount arsenal)

Auto-generated by `battle_sim.py` — seeds **2, 3, 5, 7, 11**, max rounds **50**.

Mounts from [`early-era-stat-blocks.md`](early-era-stat-blocks.md) / [`../arsenal.md`](../arsenal.md): Track → Acc → Pen → damage die. Chooser: **force picture × mission → posture** (+ thin specials). Per-ship morale, fall-back stations, and 1D battle axis (`|Δx|` → range band). Damage tables break out **plasma** vs **cannon** (and **close** for G18b) by target class.
